目标、关系、瞄准/辅助与中心；方向/自身/严格敌NPC子集可复用，友方即时效果/瞄准策略须补。；/castMovementPolicy: 施法阶段/移动/引导/蓄力/跟进及实际效果递送。；/castLifecycle: 施法阶段/移动/引导/蓄力/跟进及实际效果递送。；/effectDelivery: 施法阶段/移动/引导/蓄力/跟进及实际效果递送。；/factors/0: 因子绑定factor_deal_damage；参数=[0, 2, 0, 800, 0, 0]。此绑定仅零固定基数+ATK比例的物理/金伤公式切片；不继承目录中其它伤害类型/暴击参数缺口，实际递送/触发仍按父计划审查。现有能力仅表示可复用乘区/计划；触发、取值对象、类型和优先序仍按原定义逐项实现。；/factors/1: 因子绑定factor_apply_status_effect；参数=["effect_tribulation_paralysis", 3000, 1, "", 0, "target", "", 0, 0, 0, 15]。现有能力仅表示可复用乘区/计划；触发、取值对象、类型和优先序仍按原定义逐项实现。；/castLifecycle/version: 完整施法version规则：1；/castLifecycle/preset: 完整施法preset规则："anchored_windup"；/castLifecycle/preparation: 完整施法preparation规则：{"kind": "fixed_windup", "durationMs": 750}；/castLifecycle/active: 完整施法active规则：{"kind": "instant"}；/castLifecycle/movement: 完整施法movement规则：{"preparation": {"kind": "locked", "resumeLatestIntent": true}, "active": {"kind": "free"}}；/castLifecycle/aim: 完整施法aim规则：{"lockAt": "release", "binding": "world_point"}；/castLifecycle/activation: 完整施法activation规则：{"origin": "committed_world_point", "revalidation": "preserve_committed_point", "requireLineOfSight": true}；/castLifecycle/resources: 完整施法resources规则：{"reserveAt": "accept", "consumeAt": "commit", "cooldownStartsAt": "commit", "refundBeforeCommit": {"kind": "full"}}；/castLifecycle/interrupts: 完整施法interrupts规则：{"preparation": ["stunned", "dead", "silenced", "cancelled", "target_lost", "out_of_range", "line_of_sight", "region_changed"], "active": [], "explicitCancelDuringPreparation": true, ；/castLifecycle/recovery: 完整施法recovery规则：{"durationMs": 260, "cancelByMove": true, "cancelByExplicitAction": true}；现有共享后摇不能被缩短。；/castLifecycle/recast: 完整施法recast规则：{"kind": "none"}；/castLifecycle/indicator: 完整施法indicator规则：{"whileAiming": "editable", "whileCharging": "none", "afterRelease": "show_until_authority_rejects", "activePresentation": "runtime_owned"}|I01,I04,M02,M03,M07,P02,P03 → R3.1,R3.2,R3.4,R4.2,R6.1,R6.2|D-03,OBS-01|
